- **analysis-bot**: Emission-mode documenter - given a set of source files with no comments (author unavailable), it reads the code deeply and annotates it IN PLACE with dense, honest commentary about what the code does - observed mechanism, invariants, data flow, the apparent role of constants - with inferred purpose explicitly marked as hypothesis. - **andon-verifier**: Converts andon-defender and andon-challenger claims into reproduced facts by running deterministic checks -- tests, greps, execution -- as the fact-finding third leg of andon-verify's tribunal strategy (strategy a), so both cases rest on evidence rather than assertion. Read and execute only; never edits the artifact under review; never renders a pass/fail verdict itself. - **antislop**: Writing specialist for explicit prose-artifact intent. Two modes: (1) style mode, apply when writing, rewriting, editing, polishing, reviewing, or auditing an artifact; (2) audit mode, score text 0-100 and list every violation when asked to check, grade, or flag AI patterns. Do not activate for ordinary factual or technical explanations. ([drunkrhin0/antislop](https://github.com/drunkrhin0/antislop/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/antislop.md) · ★24)
- **Antropolog produktu**: Badacz UX prowadzi badania użytkowników: wywiady, ankiety, testy uzyteczności, persony, JTBD i mapy podrozy. Zamienia to, co ludzie realnie robią i mówią, w insighty poparte dowodami. Różni się od Researchera UX, który skautuje trendy wizualne - Badacz UX bada ludzi, nie estetyke. ([TheJacksonCode/Agent-Architecture](https://github.com/TheJacksonCode/Agent-Architecture/blob/HEAD/agents/ux_researcher.md) · ★99)
- **anvil**: Evidence-first coding agent. Verifies before presenting. Attacks its own output. Uses adversarial multi-model review, IDE diagnostics, and SQL-tracked verification to ensure code quality. ([masonbesmer/lyra-sapphire](https://github.com/masonbesmer/lyra-sapphire/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/anvil.agent.md) · ★1)
- **Anvil**: Moonshot-family code producer. Runs Kimi K2.6 (`kimi-k2.6`) via Moonshot's direct API with temperature 1 (reasoning-model default) and 256K context. Specialization — deliberate, context-wide code generation where the whole project matters. Invoked when {{PRINCIPAL_NAME}} names "Anvil", or as a Kimi-family alternative to Forge on coding tasks that benefit from long-context reasoning. Writes code; does not just review. Distinct from Forge (OpenAI-family, GPT-5.4), Cato (auditor), Engineer (Marcus Webb, Claude-family). ([theBlackEndDev/ira](https://github.com/theBlackEndDev/ira/blob/HEAD/v5/.claude/agents/Anvil.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **anvil**: Evidence-first coding agent. Verifies every change with adversarial multi-model review, SQL-tracked verification, and automatic rollback. Use for any coding task where you want proof, not promises. ([addeelnayyer/claude-anvil](https://github.com/addeelnayyer/claude-anvil/blob/HEAD/agents/anvil.agent.md))
- **anvil**: 以證據為優先的程式碼代理人（coding agent）。呈現前先驗證。使用對抗性多模型審查、IDE 診斷與 SQL 追蹤驗證來確保程式碼品質。 ([RDCrystalLab/agentic-plugins](https://github.com/RDCrystalLab/agentic-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/anvil/agents/anvil.agent.md))
